  § 121. Assembly districts. The assembly districts shall consist as
follows:
  1. First district. In the county of Suffolk, that part of the town of
Brookhaven bounded by a line described as follows: Beginning at the
intersection of Edwards Avenue South and the Brookhaven/South Hampton
town line, thence southwesterly along said line to a line extended
easterly from the East Moriches census designated place to the
Brookhaven/South Hampton town line, thence westerly along said line
extended to the line of East Moriches census designated place, thence
southwesterly along said line to the East Moriches Center Moriches
census designated place line, thence southwesterly along the Center
Moriches census designated place line to the shoreline of the Moriches
Bay, thence southwesterly along said shoreline to the creek westerly of
Fire Island National Sea Shore, thence northerly along said creek to
Mastic/Shirley census designated place line, thence northerly along said
line to the western line of the Fire Island National Seashore, thence
northwesterly along said line to a line extended northeasterly from
Aspen Road, thence southwesterly along said line to Old Church Road,
thence northwesterly along said road to Washington Avenue, thence
southwesterly along said avenue to Laurel Street, thence southerly along
said street to Wilson Avenue, thence southwesterly along said avenue to
the Shirley census designated place line, thence southwesterly along
said line to President Road, thence westerly along said road to Channel
Drive East, thence southwesterly along said drive to Margin Drive,
thence southwesterly along said drive to Robinwood Drive, thence
westerly along said drive to Smith Road, thence northerly along said
road to a line extended southerly from Smith Road to the terminus of
Smith Road, thence northerly along said line extended to Smith Road,
thence northerly along Smith Road to the Long Island Railroad Tracks,
thence northeasterly along said railroad tracks to the Mastic census
designated place line, thence northeasterly along said line to Wood
Avenue, thence northeasterly along said avenue to Madison Street, thence
northerly along said street to Bedford Avenue, thence northeasterly
along said avenue to Cumberland Street, thence southerly along said

  146. One hundred forty-sixth district. In the county of Erie, the town
of Amherst; in the county of Niagara, the town of Pendleton.
  147. One hundred forty-seventh district. In the county of Erie the

Jasper, Troupsburg, West Union.
